<h2>Features</h2>
<div class="cFeaturesTiles">
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-hourglass-start"></i>
<p>Quick set up in minutes</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>Sign up now (1 minute), go to your new website, follow the walk-through or video tutorials, customize the website and create your event. You can go live and start selling in minutes.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-th"></i>
<p>Assigned Seat &amp; General Admission</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>Powerful seating chart designer allows you to create any amphitheater style, round-table (dinner style), night club style or any combination and allows the buyer to pick their seat.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-recycle"></i>
<p>Single &amp; Recurring Events - Packages and Season Tickets</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>Simply create any single event or use the powerful scheduler to create a recurring event, classes or daily admissions.</p>
<p>Package events into packages or season tickets.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-hand-holding-usd"></i>
<p>Direct money deposit as you sell</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>Use your own payment processor or PayPal or simply get a new one so you will receive the sales money as you sell, before the event.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-tag"></i>
<p>Best rate in the industry</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>We offer best rate in the industry. </p>
<p>Premium plan: <span class="cPriceCont_DiscountRatio__Premium30">2.5</span>% + <span class="cPriceCont_CurrencySymbol">$</span><span class="cPriceCont_FlatFeePerTicket__Premium30">0</span> </p>
<p>or Standard plan: <span class="cPriceCont_DiscountRatio__BasicFree">2.9</span>%  + <span class="cPriceCont_CurrencySymbol">$</span><span class="cPriceCont_FlatFeePerTicket__BasicFree">0.49</span> </p>
<p>No quote or contract needed.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-store"></i>
<p>Ticket booth and retail location sales</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>Use the touch-friendly, POS (point of sales) page to sell tickets at your ticket booths or as many retail locations. Sell in cash, checks or credit / debit card. Print e-tickets or hard, thermal tickets and hand them out. Use credit card reader for faster operation.</p>
<p>Run your box-office with no extra equipment or use professional thermal printers and card readers.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="far fa-money-bill-alt"></i>
<p>Face-to-face sales and cash payments</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>Accept cash, check or credit / debit card in face-to-face transactions.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-phone-square"></i>
<p>Over-the-phone sales</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>You and your sales staff can sell tickets over the phone and accept credit card. The tickets can be set to either get emailed to the buyer, or put in will-call booth. </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tileContainer .hover">
<div class="tile">
<div class="front face">
<div>
<i class="fa fa-ticket-alt"></i>

</div>
</div>
<div class="back face">
<p>We support all delivery methods.</p>
<p>E-ticket (Show on your phone or print at home), will-call (Pick up at the venue or retail location), mail (post services), face-to-face (for ticket booth or retail location sales)</p>
<p>Use physical tickets or e-tickets.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="back face">
